def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(0, budget_s - 10)
    N = 4096  # maybe if time; can use 2048 if slow? choose 4096
    # initialize best
    x_best = init_profile_cos(N, a=0.5)
    best_score = exact_score(x_best, N)
    # if height_sequence available, maybe include its you? Need not.
    starts = list of profiles
    for each start with time budget per start:
        z = np.log(start + 1e-12); z -= mean
        run_full_vector_optimize(z, N, beta schedule, time_left, best, best_score)
        update best
    # upsample best to larger N for final? If best found on N, evaluate at N2=N*2 maybe and if better use that? Need get sequence of x_best on N. To evaluate at N2, we can sample the optimized discrete vector? Optimized x may have high-frequency components; upsample by nearest? Better: use optimized x as profile and create larger vector by interpolation? The optimized x is itself a function; we can use np.interp on i/N to N2 or simply evaluate a Gaussian? If we upscale linearly, sum changes. We can take x_large = np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,N2), np.linspace(0,1,N), x/N? Actually x sum1; we want sum1 at N2. If we set x_large_i = np.interp(t_i, t_old, x_old) * N2/N? Since sum x_old=1, integral approx 1/N_old? Wait x_old_i ~ f(t)/N_old. If create x_large_i = f(t)/N_large, need f = x_old*N_old. So x_large = np.interp(t, t_old, x_old * N_old) / N_large. Then normalize sum1. This preserves smooth profile. If optimized x has high-frequency features at N, interpolation may smooth. We can also use N_final = N and return. Larger N may improve if continuous profile; but if optimized high-freq, interpolation may alter. We can test both by computing score; choose better. Full vector optimize function:
`run_optimizer(z, N, beta_list, max_time, best, best_score, rng, deadline)`
- x = softmax(z) normalized.
- beta = 10 initially.
- t_iter=0.
- while time < limit:
   x = softmax(z) (clip z)
   c = full_conv_fft(x)
   y = N*c
   y_max = max(y)
   score = 2*y_max

Let's detail FFT helpers:
```
def _fft_size(length):
    n=1
    while n<length: n*=2
    return n

def full_convolution(x):
    n=x.size
    size=1
    while size < 2*n-1: size*=2
    F=np.fft.rfft(x, size=size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, size=size)[:2*n-1]
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-30]=0
    return c
```
Since x nonnegative, irfft may have tiny negative.

Hankel product:
```
def hankel_product(x, W):
    n=x.size
    u=x[::-1]
    size=1
    while size < 3*n-1: size*=2
    Fu=np.fft.rfft(u,size=size)
    FW=np.fft.rfft(W,size=size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fu*FW, size=size)
    return conv[n-1:n-1+n]  # length n
```
Check index: conv length at least 3n-1? For size FFT convolution linear length len(u)+len(W)-1 = n+(2n-1)-1=3n-1. We take indices n-1 through 2n-2 (length n). Yes.

Softmax weights for c:
```
def conv_weights(c, beta):
    cmax=np.max(c)
    if beta<=0: return np.full_like(c,1.0/len(c))
    ex=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax))
    s=ex.sum()
    if s==0 or not finite: uniform
    return ex/s
```
If beta*cmax large shift okay. Use np.exp with where? ex = np.exp(np.clip(beta*(c-cmax), -700, 0)); because c-cmax≤0.

Softmax x:
```
def softmax_weights(z, floor=0.0):
    z=z-z.max()
    z=np.clip(z, -700, 700)
    exp=np.exp(z)
    x=exp/exp.sum()
    x += floor
    x /= x.sum()
    return x
```
Floor in x after softmax may change gradient but okay. Use floor=1e-9 maybe. If floor too high adds uniform. Use 1e-10.

def score_from_conv(c, n, sum_a=None): if sum_a None use 1 for normalized; return 2*n*max(c)/(sum_a**2)
```

def profile_cos(N, a, shift=0.0):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N)
    x = 1.0 + a*np.cos(np.pi*t)
    x=np.maximum(x,1e-6)
    return x/x.sum()
```

def optimize_full(z, N, deadline, best_x, best_score, max_iter, beta_start=20, beta_max=2000, label=""):
    local_x = z_to_x(z,N)
    local_score = score_conv(full_conv(local_x),N)
    if local_score < best_score: update
    beta = beta_start
    best_local = local_x.copy(); best_local_score=local_score
    stale=0
    it=0
    while time<deadline and it<max_iter:
       c=full_conv(x)  # use x updated from z at end; but avoid recompute? We have x.

           if local_score < best_local_score: update best_local; if < best_score: global
       else:
           stale+=1

               # try lower/raise beta

                  beta=beta_start; stale=0
       it+=1
```

Block local search:
```
def block_local_search(x, N, deadline, best_x, best_score, rng):
    current=x.copy(); score=exact...
    while time<deadline:
       # random block scaling
       start = rng.integers(0,N); width = rng.integers(2, max(3,N//20))? For N 4096, width 50-500.
       cand=current.copy()
       cand[start:start+width] *= exp(rng.normal(0,0.1))
       # maybe rotate? no
       normalize(cand)
       scorec=exact_score
       if scorec < score: current=cand; score=scorec; update best
       else large no_improve reduce? 
```

Adding constant to z does not change; z-ηd + const = z -η(d-const). Setting d=d - mean(d) adds const*?     # z is log weights (centered) of length N, x=softmax(z)

    local_best_x = local_x.copy(); local_best_score=local_score
    if local_score < best_score: best_x=local_x.copy(); best_score=local_score
    beta = 8.0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       iters += 1
       x = local_x
       y = N*c  # c from previous? We have c from previous x. At first after candidate, c computed.
       # If we didn't update x? We'll compute c at top to be safe.
```

Let's code `make_direction(x, y, beta, soft_weight)`:
```
def make_direction(x, y, beta, soft_weight):
    n=y.size? len 2n-1
    ymean = y.mean()
    dev = y - ymean
    dscale = np.max(np.abs(dev))
    W = np.zeros_like(y)
    if dscale > 1e-14: W += dev / dscale
    Ws = conv_weights(y, beta)
    W += soft_weight * Ws
    G = 2.0 * hankel_matrix_vec(x, W)
    G = G - G.mean()
    gscale = np.max(np.abs(G))
    if gscale < 1e-14: return None
    return G / gscale
```
`conv_weights` with beta; if beta small, weights nearly uniform. `beta` on y scale O(1); beta=5 okay. For beta=0, uniform.

In optimizer, choose `soft_weight` maybe 0.5 for beta high, 0.1 low. Let's define per beta:
`soft_weight = 0.1 + 0.5 * (beta/100)` capped 1? But if beta high, W_soft sparse, its mass at max; adding with weight maybe 0.5. If beta low, W_soft nearly uniform, adding uniform constant to W, mean subtraction removes? Uniform constant over lags not constant in G? W uniform yields G_i=2∑_{valid} x_j * 1/L =2x/L, roughly constant for interior; mean subtraction removes; not useful. So beta low flat term enough.

`p2ge`:
```
def pow2_at_least(m):
    n=1
    while n<m: n <<=1
    return n
```

`fft_conv_square`:
```
def fft_conv_square(x):
    n=x.size
    size=pow2_at_least(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(x, size=size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, size=size)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c[c<0]=0.0
    return c
```
Maybe c sum = S^2; with sum1, but FFT numerical maybe sum 1.

`hankel_matrix_vec`:
```
def hankel_matrix_vec(x,W):
    n=x.size
    size=pow2_at_least(3*n-1)
    u=x[::-1]
    Fu=np.fft.rfft(u,size=size)
    FW=np.fft.rfft(W,size=size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fu*FW,size=size)
    return conv[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Conv may tiny imaginary discarded. W length 2n-1. Good.

`softmax_dist`:
```
def softmax_dist(z, floor=0.0):
    z = z - np.max(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    x=e/e.sum()
    if floor>0:
       x = x*(1.0 - len(x)*floor) + floor? This forces sum. Simpler: x=e; x=np.maximum(x, floor); x/=x.sum()
```
Using floor: `x = np.maximum(e, floor); x /= x.sum()`. If floor=1e-12. Good. Keep z not too extreme? We clip z before, so e min e^-50; floor maybe not needed. Use floor=0.0.

`conv_weights_y(y,beta)`:
```
def conv_weights_y(y,beta):
    if beta <= 0:
       return np.full(y.shape, 1.0/y.size)
    ym = y.max()
    z = beta*(y-ym)
    z = np.clip(z, -700, 0)
    e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: uniform
    return e/s
```

```
def optimize_full_vector(z, N, max_seconds, hard_deadline, best_x, best_score, rng, label):
    deadline = min(hard_deadline, time.monotonic()+max_seconds)
    z = np.asarray(z,float); z -= z.mean(); z = np.clip(z,-12,12)
    x = softmax_dist(z)
    c = fft_conv_square(x)
    y = N*c
    score = 2.0*float(np.max(y))
    var = float(np.var(y))
    local_best_x = x.copy(); local_best_score=score; local_best_var=var
    if score < best_score: best_x=x.copy(); best_score=score
    beta = 8.0
    beta_steps = [8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024]
    beta_idx=0
    soft_weight = 0.1
    stale=0
    it=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    var_penalty=0.08
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it +=1
        if it % 50 ==0 and time.monotonic()-last_print > 30: print...
        surrogate = score + var_penalty*var
        d = make_direction(x,y,beta,soft_weight)
        if d is None:
           stale +=1
           if stale > 50: break
           continue
        base_scale = 0.3
        if beta > 100: base_scale=0.15
        scales = [base_scale, base_scale*0.4, base_scale*2.5, base_scale*0.1]
        best_surch = surrogate
        best_candidate = None
        for sc in scales:
           if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
           zt = z - sc*d
           zt -= zt.mean()
           zt = np.clip(zt, -12,12)
           xt = softmax_dist(zt)
           ct = fft_conv_square(xt)
           yt = N*ct
           st = 2.0*float(np.max(yt))
           vt = float(np.var(yt))
           sur = st + var_penalty*vt
           if sur < best_surch - 1e-10:
               best_surch=sur; best_candidate=(zt,xt,ct,yt,st,vt)
        if best_candidate is not None:
           z,xt,ct,yt,st,vt = best_candidate
           x=xt; c=ct; y=yt; score=st; var=vt; stale=0
           if score < local_best_score:
               local_best_x=x.copy(); local_best_score=score; local_best_var=var
           if score < best_score:
               best_x=x.copy(); best_score=score
               print('improved', label)
        else:
           stale +=1
           if stale > 30:
               # increase beta/soft
               if beta_idx < len(beta_steps)-1:
                   beta_idx +=1; beta = beta_steps[beta_idx]; soft_weight = min(1.0, 0.1 + 0.2*beta_idx); stale=0
               else:
                   # perturb current best local
                   pert = 0.05 + 0.05*rng.random()
                   z = np.log(local_best_x + 1e-12) + pert*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,N)
                   z -= z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-12,12)
                   x=softmax_dist(z); c=fft_conv_square(x); y=N*c; score=2*max(y); var=var(y)
                   if score > local_best_score + 0.02:
                       z=np.log(local_best_x+1e-12); z-=z.mean(); x=local_best_x.copy(); c=fft_conv_square(x); y=N*c; score=local_best_score; var=...
                   beta=beta_steps[0]; beta_idx=0; soft_weight=0.1; stale=0
        if it % 100 ==0:
             pass
    # final ensure best updated
    return best_x,best_score
```

Block search implementation:
```
def block_local_search(x,N,deadline,best_x,best_score,rng, label="block"):
    current=x.copy(); c=fft_conv_square(current); current_score=2*N*np.max(c)
    no=0
    print label
    # width range random
    while time<deadline:
       if no>200: break? maybe reduce noise
       start=int(rng.integers(0, 2*N))? But block within [0,N). width=int(rng.integers(3, max(4,N//30)))
       s=int(rng.integers(0, N-width+1))
       cand=current.copy()
       scale=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08 + 0.04*(no//100)))
       cand[s:s+width]*=scale
       cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0); cand/=cand.sum()
       cc=fft_conv_square(cand); sc=2*N*cc.max()
       if sc < current_score - 1e-12:
          current=cand; current_score=sc; no=0
          if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_x=cand.copy(); print
       else:
          no+=1
          if no%50==0: # occasionally reset to best
             if best_score < current_score:
                current=best_x.copy(); current_score=best_score; no=0
    return best_x,best_score
```
Could be slow but okay. Use max 2000 iterations? Time controls.

```
def random_trig_start(N, rng, K=12):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N)
    z=np.zeros(N)
    coeff = [0.0]*K
    for r in range(1,K+1):
        coeff[r-1] = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6/np.sqrt(r)))
        # bias odd modes positive? maybe for r=1 add 0.4
    if K>=1: coeff[0]+=0.4
    for idx in range(K):
        if abs(coeff[idx])>1e-8:
            z += coeff[idx]*np.cos((idx+1)*np.pi*t)
    z = z/ (np.std(z)+1e-12)
    return softmax_dist(z)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

"""
Approximate flat positive convolution search.

The score is scale-invariant, so we optimize nonnegative "probabilities"
x >= 0, sum(x)=1, and minimize 2*n*max(x*x).  The search uses:
  * FFT convolution and an exact scoring objective,
  * a stochastic gradient direction combining variance reduction of the
    convolution coefficients and a softmax focus on current peaks,
  * exact line-search acceptances,
  * several deterministic and random trigonometric starts,
  * final upsampling and a short block-perturbation polish.
"""


def _pow2_at_least(m: int) -> int:
    n = 1
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n


def _fft_conv_square(x: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Full convolution x*x for nonnegative real x, using FFT."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    size = _pow2_at_least(2 * n - 1)
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, size=size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fx * fx, size=size)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    # Remove tiny negative roundoff; true values are nonnegative for x >= 0.
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def _hankel_matrix_vec(x: np.ndarray, W: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Compute y_i = sum_j x_j W_{i+j}, i=0..n-1, where W has length 2n-1.

    This is the structure needed for derivatives of full convolution
    coefficients with respect to x.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    W = np.asarray(W, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    size = _pow2_at_least(3 * n - 1)
    u = x[::-1]
    fu = np.fft.rfft(u, size=size)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(W, size=size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fu * fw, size=size)
    return np.asarray(conv[n - 1:n - 1 + n], dtype=np.float64)


def _softmax_dist(z: np.ndarray, floor: float = 0.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Stable softmax to a probability vector."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=12.0, neginf=-12.0)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    if floor > 0.0:
        e = np.maximum(e, floor)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(e.shape, 1.0 / e.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x = e / s
    return np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)


def _conv_peak_weights(y: np.ndarray, beta: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """Softmax weights over convolution coefficients y, emphasizing peaks."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    m = y.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    ym = float(np.max(y))
    z = beta * (y - ym)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _make_direction(x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray, beta: float, soft_weight: float):
    """
    Heuristic descent direction in log-x.

    It combines:
      * variance reduction: W_flat = y - mean(y)
      * peak concentration: W_peak = softmax(beta*y)
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0 or y.size == 0:
        return None

    ymean = float(np.mean(y))
    dev = y - ymean
    dscale = float(np.max(np.abs(dev)))
    W = np.zeros_like(y, dtype=np.float64)

    if dscale > 1e-14:
        W += dev / dscale

    Wp = _conv_peak_weights(y, beta)
    W += float(soft_weight) * Wp

    G = 2.0 * _hankel_matrix_vec(x, W)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
        return None

    # A constant shift of the direction does not change the softmax after
    # normalization; use simple mean for numerical centering.
    G = G - float(np.mean(G))
    gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
    if not np.isfinite(gscale) or gscale <= 1e-14:
        return None
    return G / gscale


def _score_from_conv(c: np.ndarray, n: int) -> float:
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def _score_x(x: np.ndarray):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    c = _fft_conv_square(x)
    return _score_from_conv(c, n), c


def _profile_one_cos(n: int, a: float = 0.5) -> np.ndarray:
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = 1.0 + float(a) * np.cos(np.pi * t)
    x = np.maximum(x, 1e-8)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return x / s


def _profile_trig(n: int, coeff: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    x(t) = softmax(sum_{r=1}^K coeff[r-1] cos(r*pi*t)).
    This family includes useful antisymmetric oscillations around 1/2.
    """
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    coeff = np.asarray(coeff, dtype=np.float64)
    for r, cval in enumerate(coeff):
        if abs(cval) > 1e-12:
            z += float(cval) * np.cos((r + 1) * np.pi * t)
    std = float(np.std(z))
    if std > 1e-12:
        z /= std
    return _softmax_dist(z)


def _random_trig_start(n: int, rng: np.random.Generator, K: int = 12) -> np.ndarray:
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(1, K + 1):
        amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.55 / math.sqrt(r)))
        # Bias the fundamental antisymmetric mode toward useful asymmetry.
        if r == 1:
            amp += 0.35 + 0.25 * float(rng.random())
        z += amp * np.cos(r * np.pi * t)
    std = float(np.std(z))
    if std > 1e-12:
        z /= std
    return _softmax_dist(z)


def _upsample_profile(old_x: np.ndarray, new_n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    old_x = np.asarray(old_x, dtype=np.float64)
    old_n = old_x.size
    if old_n == new_n:
        x = old_x.copy()
        s = float(np.sum(x))
        if s > 0.0:
            x /= s
        return x

    # Recover an approximate density f(t): f(i/old_n) ~ old_x[i] * old_n.
    old_t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    new_t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.interp(new_t, old_t, old_x * float(old_n), left=float(old_x[0] * old_n), right=float(old_x[-1] * old_n))
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(f))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(new_n, 1.0 / new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    return f / s


def _optimize_full_vector(
    z0: np.ndarray,
    n: int,
    max_seconds: float,
    hard_deadline: float,
    best_x: np.ndarray,
    best_score: float,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
    label: str,
):
    """
    Optimize log-weights z for fixed length n using exact line search.

    The direction is heuristic but validated by the exact score; surrogate
    accepts are allowed only when a variance/score surrogate improves.
    """
    now = time.monotonic()
    deadline = min(float(hard_deadline), now + float(max_seconds))
    if deadline <= now:
        return best_x, best_score

    z = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=8.0, neginf=-8.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -12.0, 12.0)

    x = _softmax_dist(z)
    c = _fft_conv_square(x)
    y = n * c
    score = float(2.0 * np.max(y))
    var = float(np.var(y))

    local_best_x = x.copy()
    local_best_score = score

    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
        best_x = x.copy()
        best_score = score
        print(f"[{label}] start score {score:.6f}")

    beta_steps = [6.0, 12.0, 24.0, 48.0, 96.0, 192.0, 384.0, 768.0, 1536.0]
    beta_idx = 0
    beta = beta_steps[0]
    soft_weight = 0.25
    stale = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        if it % 100 == 0:
            if time.monotonic() - last_print > 25.0:
                print(
                    f"[{label}] it={it} beta={beta:.0f} score={score:.6f} "
                    f"best={best_score:.6f} time={time.monotonic() - now:.0f}s"
                )
                last_print = time.monotonic()

        # If we have wandered far from the known global best, recenter.
        if score > best_score + 0.12:
            z = np.log(np.asarray(best_x, dtype=np.float64) + 1e-12)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            z = np.clip(z, -12.0, 12.0)
            x = _softmax_dist(z)
            c = _fft_conv_square(x)
            y = n * c
            score = float(2.0 * np.max(y))
            var = float(np.var(y))
            stale = 0
            continue

        surrogate = score + max(0.05, 0.8 * (20.0 / (beta + 20.0))) * var

        d = _make_direction(x, y, beta, soft_weight)
        if d is None:
            stale += 1
            if stale > 120:
                break
            continue

        base = 0.45 if beta < 20.0 else 1.0 / math.sqrt(max(beta, 1.0))
        if base <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(base):
            base = 0.1
        scales = (base, base * 0.35, base * 2.2)

        best_surr = surrogate
        best_candidate = None

        for sc in scales:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            if sc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            zt = z - sc * d
            zt = zt - float(np.mean(zt))
            zt = np.clip(zt, -12.0, 12.0)
            xt = _softmax_dist(zt)
            ct = _fft_conv_square(xt)
            yt = n * ct
            st = float(2.0 * np.max(yt))
            vt = float(np.var(yt))
            pen = max(0.05, 0.8 * (20.0 / (beta + 20.0)))
            sur = st + pen * vt
            if np.isfinite(sur) and sur < best_surr - 1e-11:
                best_surr = sur
                best_candidate = (zt, xt, ct, yt, st, vt)

        if best_candidate is not None:
            z, x, c, y, score, var = best_candidate
            stale = 0
            if score < local_best_score:
                local_best_x = x.copy()
                local_best_score = score
            if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                best_x = x.copy()
                best_score = score
                print(f"[{label}] improved score {score:.6f} beta={beta:.0f}")
            continue

        stale += 1
        stale_limit = 70 if beta < 256.0 else 140
        if stale >= stale_limit:
            if beta_idx + 1 < len(beta_steps):
                beta_idx += 1
                beta = beta_steps[beta_idx]
                soft_weight = min(2.0, 0.25 + 0.25 * beta_idx)
                stale = 0
                continue

            # Restart near the best known point, with a modest perturbation.
            if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score < local_best_score:
                ref_x = np.asarray(best_x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                ref_score = float(best_score)
            else:
                ref_x = local_best_x.copy()
                ref_score = float(local_best_score)

            zbase = np.log(ref_x + 1e-12)
            zbase -= float(np.mean(zbase))
            noise = (0.03 + 0.04 * float(rng.random())) * rng.standard_normal(n)
            znoisy = zbase + noise
            znoisy -= float(np.mean(znoisy))
            znoisy = np.clip(znoisy, -12.0, 12.0)
            xnoisy = _softmax_dist(znoisy)
            cnoisy = _fft_conv_square(xnoisy)
            ynoisy = n * cnoisy
            snoisy = float(2.0 * np.max(ynoisy))
            vnoisy = float(np.var(ynoisy))

            if np.isfinite(snoisy) and snoisy < ref_score + 0.06:
                z = znoisy
                x = xnoisy
                c = cnoisy
                y = ynoisy
                score = snoisy
                var = vnoisy
            else:
                z = zbase
                x = ref_x.copy()
                c = _fft_conv_square(x)
                y = n * c
                score = float(2.0 * np.max(y))
                var = float(np.var(y))

            beta_idx = 0
            beta = beta_steps[0]
            soft_weight = 0.25
            stale = 0

    # Ensure the caller sees a valid best.
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_x is None or best_x.size == 0:
        fx = _profile_one_cos(n, 0.5)
        best_x = fx
        best_score = _score_x(fx)[0]
    return best_x, best_score


def _block_local_search(
    x0: np.ndarray,
    n: int,
    deadline: float,
    best_x: np.ndarray,
    best_score: float,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
    label: str,
):
    """Short multiplicative block perturbations from the current best."""
    current = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if current.size != n:
        current = _upsample_profile(current, n)
    s = float(np.sum(current))
    if s <= 0.0:
        current = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        current /= s

    c = _fft_conv_square(current)
    cur_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score:
        best_x = current.copy()
        best_score = cur_score
        print(f"[{label}] polish start {cur_score:.6f}")

    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()
    min_w = 3
    max_w = max(8, n // 24)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if it % 200 == 0:
            if time.monotonic() - last_print > 25.0:
                print(f"[{label}] polish it={it} score={cur_score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")
                last_print = time.monotonic()

        if no_improve > 1500:
            break

        w = int(rng.integers(min_w, max_w + 1))
        if w >= n:
            w = max(2, n // 10)
        st = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        box = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.07 + 0.02 * (1.0 / (1.0 + no_improve))))
        cand = current.copy()
        cand[st:st + w] *= box
        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
        cs = float(np.sum(cand))
        if cs <= 0.0:
            no_improve += 1
            continue
        cand /= cs

        cc = _fft_conv_square(cand)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(cc))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
            current = cand
            cur_score = sc
            no_improve = 0
            if sc < best_score:
                best_x = cand.copy()
                best_score = sc
                print(f"[{label}] polish improved {sc:.6f}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 0 and no_improve % 80 == 0 and np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score < cur_score + 1e-9:
                current = np.asarray(best_x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                if current.size != n:
                    current = _upsample_profile(current, n)
                c = _fft_conv_square(current)
                cur_score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(c))))
                no_improve = 0

    if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score:
        best_x = current.copy()
        best_score = cur_score
    return best_x, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing:
        2 * n * max(convolve(sequence, sequence)) / (sum(sequence)**2).

    Returns a finite nonnegative Python list.
    """
    try:
        if budget_s is None:
            budget = float(kwargs.get("budget_s", 2400.0))
        else:
            budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 30.0:
        x = _profile_one_cos(2048, 0.5)
        return [float(v) for v in x]

    try:
        seed_val = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        if "seed" in kwargs:
            seed_val = int(kwargs.get("seed", 42))
        else:
            seed_val = 42
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_val)

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start_time + max(0.0, budget - 6.0)
    n = 4096

    # A very safe fallback, better than the previous uniform-fixed-score 2.0.
    best_x = _profile_one_cos(n, 0.5)
    best_score, _ = _score_x(best_x)

    # If there is an existing construction in the environment, score it too.
    try:
        parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if parent is not None:
            px = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
            px = np.clip(px, 0.0, 1000.0)
            ps = float(np.sum(px))
            if np.all(np.isfinite(px)) and px.size > 0 and ps >= 0.01:
                pxn = px / ps
                pscore, _ = _score_x(pxn)
                if np.isfinite(pscore) and pscore < best_score:
                    best_x = pxn.copy()
                    best_score = pscore
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"initial score {best_score:.6f}")

    # A simple deterministic goal.  We only use it to avoid wasting all the
    # remaining start budget after we are already clearly below the target.
    goal = 1.5027

    starts = []
    labels = []
    starts.append(_profile_one_cos(n, 0.45)); labels.append("cos45")
    starts.append(_profile_one_cos(n, 0.65)); labels.append("cos65")

    coeff_a = np.array([0.55, -0.30, 0.22, -0.12, 0.14, 0.05], dtype=np.float64)
    starts.append(_profile_trig(n, coeff_a)); labels.append("trigA")

    starts.append(_random_trig_start(n, rng, 12)); labels.append("rand12a")
    starts.append(_random_trig_start(n, rng, 16)); labels.append("rand16a")

    allocs = [650.0, 300.0, 300.0, 250.0, 250.0]

    for i, sx in enumerate(starts):
        remain = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remain < 260.0:
            break
        if best_score < goal + 0.015:
            break
        sec = min(float(allocs[i]), remain - 260.0)
        if sec < 30.0:
            break
        z = np.log(np.asarray(sx, dtype=np.float64) + 1e-12)
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        z = np.clip(z, -12.0, 12.0)
        best_x, best_score = _optimize_full_vector(
            z, n, sec, hard_deadline, best_x, best_score, rng, labels[i]
        )

    # Continue polishing the best sequence found so far for the remaining time.
    remain = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 220.0:
        z = np.log(np.asarray(best_x, dtype=np.float64) + 1e-12)
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        z = np.clip(z, -12.0, 12.0)
        sec = min(1100.0, remain - 170.0)
        if sec > 60.0:
            best_x, best_score = _optimize_full_vector(
                z, n, sec, hard_deadline, best_x, best_score, rng, "refine"
            )

    # Consider upsampling the best profile.  Longer sequences often improve the
    # discrete approximation of the continuum profile, but we keep the best.
    final_x = np.asarray(best_x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    final_len = final_x.size
    final_score = best_score

    candidate_lengths = []
    if final_len < 5582:
        candidate_lengths.append(5582)
    if final_len < 8192:
        candidate_lengths.append(8192)

    for target_n in candidate_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 30.0:
            break
        xx = _upsample_profile(final_x, target_n)
        sc, _ = _score_x(xx)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
            final_x = xx
            final_len = target_n
            final_score = sc
            print(f"upsampled to {target_n}: score {final_score:.6f}")

    # Short block-polish on the final sequence.
    remain = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 70.0:
        polish_deadline = time.monotonic() + min(350.0, remain - 12.0)
        final_x, final_score = _block_local_search(
            final_x, final_len, polish_deadline, final_x, final_score, rng, "polish"
        )

    # Final sanitization.
    final_x = np.asarray(final_x, dtype=np.float64)
    final_x = np.clip(final_x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(final_x))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        final_x = _profile_one_cos(2048, 0.5)
        s = float(np.sum(final_x))
    final_x /= s

    # Optional cross-check against the provided evaluator.  If available and
    # slightly better, we still return the same valid sanitized list.
    try:
        checker = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
        if checker is not None:
            seq_list = [float(v) for v in final_x]
            checker_score = checker(seq_list)
            if np.isfinite(checker_score) and checker_score < final_score:
                final_score = float(checker_score)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"final score own {final_score:.6f} length {final_x.size}")
    return [float(v) for v in final_x]
```
